# WoW Forever dungeon quest prerequisites — through level 59

> **Never arrive without the quest.** Check the pickup, finish the prerequisites, then head to the dungeon.

Updated: 2026-11-14

471 unique quests across 35 dungeon sections (522 section entries). Repeated quest IDs across dungeon wings are intentional; completing one ID completes that quest everywhere.

## Scope and evidence

All 35 dungeon/wing sections, including Forever’s nine new dungeons. Includes dungeon objectives, prerequisite deliveries, in-dungeon starters and post-run follow-ups with documented minimum acceptance level below 60. Quest-level-60 entries remain in scope when obtainable by 59; explicit minimum-60 entries are excluded. Alliance/Warlock filters are the default, with Horde and other classes included and labeled. Unknown acceptance levels remain visible.

WoW Forever can differ from other versions of WoW. Minimum acceptance level is not quest level. Confirm uncertain requirements in game using the linked evidence. This guide is not an eligibility guarantee.

- Compiled from public Forever quest records, dungeon guides, Blizzard notes and Questie observed quest-relation traces; not personally verified by running every quest in the live game.
- Required = finish the stated prerequisite first. Optional breadcrumb = a referral, not a documented hard gate. None documented = no required earlier quest found in consulted sources, NOT proof that none exists. Unknown = source gaps, conflicts or uncertain availability.
- Observed Questie traces infer prerequisites from player sessions and distinguish AND groups from alternative prerequisites. They are useful evidence, not authoritative server metadata. Missing trace fields are not evidence of no prerequisite.
- Classic-origin quest details remain provisional when no current Forever record settles them. Where locations or chains conflict, the discrepancy is stated instead of inventing a clean answer.
- Database-only and possible Season of Discovery carryover quests are explicitly marked Unknown, not promised available. Some level-58/59 dungeon-set upgrade chains also have disputed live availability.
- Coordinates are approximate and map-specific. Prefer the NPC, named settlement and zone over old Classic coordinates on redrawn Forever maps.
- Before-travel lists contain explicitly labeled faction/class errands; only do the entries applicable to your character. The quest list itself follows your chosen faction and class filters.
- Important corrections: Stockade The Fury Runs Deep needs The Dark Iron War in Dun Modr, Wetlands (prerequisite min25). Dalaran Alliance quests should be prepared through An Alarming Request in Southshore (min30), rather than trusting contradictory optional-breadcrumb advice.

Dataset sources: [1. mmonster.co](<https://mmonster.co/wow-forever/guides/dungeons/quests>) · [2. bytetavern.com](<https://bytetavern.com/wow-forever-dungeon-quests/>) · [3. raw.githubusercontent.com](<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Questie/QuestieDB/master/src/corrections/Forever/traces/foreverQuestRelationTraces.lua>) · [4. wowf.io](<https://wowf.io/en/dungeons>) · [5. db.icy-veins.com](<https://db.icy-veins.com/wow-forever/quests>)

## Legend and use

- **Required** — complete the documented prerequisite chain before the target quest becomes available.
- **Optional breadcrumb** — a lead-in or routing quest, not a documented hard gate.
- **None documented** — no prerequisite was found in the consulted sources; this does not prove that none exists.
- **Unknown** — the requirement is unresolved or conflicting; confirm in game.
- **Min. ?** — minimum acceptance level is unconfirmed. The interactive guide keeps these quests visible at every level cap.
- **Faction / class** — class-only restrictions apply to both factions unless a faction is explicitly included. Unrestricted quests apply to every class of the named faction.
- Complete each dungeon’s **Before you travel** checklist, then collect the target quests. Checkmarks in this Markdown copy are independent of the browser checklist.
- The [interactive checklist](./) defaults to Alliance, Warlock, and maximum acceptance level 59. It supports search, filters, and browser-local progress keyed by unique quest ID. This Markdown guide contains every dataset entry, without those filters.

## Hall of Thanes

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: the mandatory dungeon chain is Dark Iron Map from Dark Iron Spies at Ironband's Compound, eastern Dun Morogh → Underground Map 96391 → Earthseer Farsen above Gol'Bolar Quarry in the Tundrid Hills → Old Ironforge Incursion 96393. Do this before travelling to Ironforge.
- [ ] Optional orientation route (not a proven gate for the map): Beldin Steelgrill at Steelgrill's Depot near Kharanos, Dun Morogh → A Visitor to Dun Morogh 96408 → Earthseer Farsen's Farsen's Watch 96392 → Nip 'Em in the Bud 96390. Kill the spies and watch for the map. Direct quest records separate this three-step line from the two-step map line, contrary to the older ByteTavern summary that makes all five mandatory.
- [ ] Alliance: on the descent from the High Seat into Old Ironforge, pick up The Restless Dead from Afadra Dunwall above the gate, and Important Heirlooms from Thom Filch on the bridge. Inside: Ghostly Attendant in the second room; Treaty tablet beyond the vault door after the final boss.
- [ ] Horde: An Ancient Grudge is listed for both factions. Important Heirlooms is Both in the index and reportedly offered to Horde in Mmonster, but WOWF.IO calls it Alliance-only; retain it as disputed, not a guaranteed Horde pickup. Reaching these NPCs requires entering hostile Ironforge.

<a id="ragefire-chasm"></a>

## Ragefire Chasm

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: no faction or class quest is documented here; all ten indexed rows below are Horde-only.
- [ ] Horde: Thrall, Grommash Hold in Orgrimmar, starts Hidden Enemies 5726. Get a Lieutenant's Insignia from Burning Blade cultists in Skull Rock, Durotar → return to Thrall → 5727: show it to Neeru Fireblade in Orgrimmar's Cleft of Shadow, then report to Thrall → take dungeon kill step 5728 before entering.
- [ ] Horde: collect both Rahauro quests on Thunder Bluff's Elder Rise; The Power to Destroy... from Varimathras in Undercity's Royal Quarter; Slaying the Beast from Neeru Fireblade near the portal. Inside, inspect Maur Grimtotem's corpse near Oggleflint.

<a id="ruins-of-lordaeron"></a>

## Ruins of Lordaeron

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: no external prerequisite is documented for the four dungeon-starting quest lines. Pick up Abominable Creatures from Captain Truman just left of the entrance; loot Bloodied Insignia, the randomly placed Crest, and Edward Heartweaver's scrawlings/crumpled paper by his body near Rath'mael in the graveyard. Keep the letter for Stormwind and its Duskwood follow-up.
- [ ] Horde — required before the run: Deathguard Linnea at the camp on the Brill–Undercity road, Tirisfal Glades (about 65.5,60.3): Rear Guard Patrol (356; kill 8 Bleeding Horrors and 8 Wandering Spirits) → Rear Guard Patrol (99156; kill elite Riptear near Venomweb Vale and return his heart) → The Wrath of Rath'mael (92422) from Deathguard Kristof beside Linnea. Required on live, unlike early beta guides.
- [ ] Horde — required before the run: Apothecary Renferrel, The Sepulcher, Silverpine Forest: A Recipe For Death (447; bear hearts and skittering blood, turn in to Master Apothecary Faranell in Undercity's Apothecarium) → A Recipe For Death (450; return to Renferrel) → A Recipe For Death (451; lake moss and Hardened Tumor, turn in to Faranell) → The New Plague (95216), Theodore Griffs in the Apothecarium. Required on live; this is the Silverpine Recipe chain, not the similarly named Tirisfal New Plague chain.
- [ ] Horde: also collect Light's Justice from Morbin Lightbane in Undercity's Royal Quarter and A Frightened Request from Tabitha Heartweaver in the Sepulcher graveyard, Silverpine. Loot The Baron's Abominable Head and the Crest inside.
- [ ] Minimum-level conflict: Blizzard's November 2 update explicitly made ALL Ruins dungeon quests minimum 15 (previously 15/16) and adjusted prerequisites. This section uses 15 rather than copying the index/WOWF.IO's stale 16s. Quest level 21/22 is not acceptance level. Some database pages still contradict the official change.

<a id="wailing-caverns"></a>

## Wailing Caverns

### Before you travel

- [ ] Both factions: finish Raptor Horns (865) from Mebok Mizzyrix in Ratchet, The Barrens — gather 5 Intact Raptor Horns from Sunscale Scytheclaws — then accept Smart Drinks (1491). This is REQUIRED, not a breadcrumb. Also take Trouble at the Docks from Crane Operator Bigglefuzz in Ratchet.
- [ ] Both factions: climb to the small cave ABOVE the skull-shaped entrance at Lushwater Oasis, The Barrens; Nalpak gives Deviate Hides and Ebru gives Deviate Eradication. Do not walk straight into the lower cave and miss them.
- [ ] Horde — full required outside chain: Start with Tonga Runetotem at the Crossroads, The Barrens: The Barrens Oases (886) → The Forgotten Pools (870) → The Stagnant Oasis (877) → Altered Beings (880) → Hamuul Runetotem (1489; travel to Arch Druid Hamuul on Thunder Bluff's Elder Rise) → Nara Wildmane (1490; nearby on Elder Rise) → Leaders of the Fang (914).
- [ ] Horde: Apothecary Zamah in the Pools of Vision cave beneath Spirit Rise, Thunder Bluff, gives Serpentbloom. No Herbalism requirement is documented for gathering these quest plants.
- [ ] Inside: after killing the four Fanglords, return to the Disciple of Naralex and complete the awakening/escort event to reach Mutanus. Loot his Glowing Shard; no external quest chain is required to start this item quest. Afterwards ask Sputtervalve in Ratchet, then find Falla Sagewind on the hill above Wailing Caverns for the faction-specific In Nightmares follow-up.

<a id="the-deadmines"></a>

## The Deadmines

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance — required Defias prework: Start The Defias Brotherhood (65) with Gryan Stoutmantle in the Sentinel Hill tower, Westfall → Wiley the Black in Lakeshire, Redridge → return to Gryan (132) → carry Wiley's Note to Master Mathias Shaw, SI:7, Old Town, Stormwind (135) → return to Gryan (141) → intercept Defias Messenger in Westfall and return his message (142) → escort the Defias Traitor from Sentinel Hill to Moonbrook and report back (155). This unlocks both the VanCleef dungeon quest 166 and Scout Riell's Red Silk Bandanas 214; do not wait until the dungeon door to start it.
- [ ] Alliance — new Forever chain, not a direct explosives pickup: Start Testing the Wells (92742; level 9) at Alba Fairmoon, Sentinel Hill inn, Westfall → Murloc Gills (92744) → The State of the Mines (92745) → Moonbrook Espionage (92747) → Explosive Consultation (92748), to Sprite Jumpsprocket in Stormwind's Dwarven District → A Dynamite Plan (92749; current direct record says 10 Rough Dynamite, obtainable by trade/AH; no Engineering requirement) → Detonation at a Distance (92750), to Jasper Fel at SI:7 in Old Town → Detonation at a Distance (92751), return Remote Detonator Kit to Sprite → Explosive Consultation (92752), return to Alba → Destruction in Deadmines (92753). Keep the supplied explosives; plant them at the hidden forge, then meet Alba in the hills at the dungeon exit and do detonator follow-up 92819.
- [ ] Alliance: Wilder Thistlenettle in Stormwind's Dwarven District gives Collecting Memories and Oh Brother...; both are completed in the OUTER mine before the instance portal. Shoni the Shilent in the same district gives Underground Assault directly. Speak with Shoni (2041) from Gnoarn in Ironforge's Tinker Town is an OPTIONAL breadcrumb, not a compulsory Ironforge detour.
- [ ] Alliance Paladins, level 20: obtain The Tome of Valor (1649; trainer/book), see Duthorian Rall in Stormwind's Cathedral of Light → The Tome of Valor (1650) to Daphne Stilwell at the southern end of Longshore, Westfall → defend Daphne (1651) → report to Duthorian (1652) → The Test of Righteousness (1653) to Jordan Stilwell outside Ironforge, Dun Morogh → accept material quest 1654. The four materials are Whitestone Oak Lumber (Deadmines Goblin Woodcarvers), Jordan's Smithing Hammer (SFK stables), Jordan's Refined Ore Shipment (Bailor's Ore Shipment 1655, Bailor Stonehand in Thelsamar, Loch Modan; retrieve ore at Mo'grosh Stronghold), and Purified Kor Gem (Seeking the Kor Gem 1442).
- [ ] Horde Paladins: start Diplomatic Incident (91858; level 18) at Danitha Morr, Bandarion Keep in western Tirisfal Glades → A Curious Pair (91859; Trevan Rol, Sepulcher crypt, Silverpine) → A Grim Fate (91860; Deathguard Baldren, Sepulcher) → Into Fenris Keep (91861; Charred Remains on Fenris Isle) → Lumina Windsinger (91862; unlock her cage in Fenris Keep cellar) → escort The Windshaper's Wrath (96204) → The Debt (95034; Lumina back at the Sepulcher) → A Moon-Kissed Blade (95036; level 20, Lumina gives it, Trevan supplies Weapon Notes and receives materials). Collect Whitestone Oak Lumber in Deadmines, Enchanted Silver Ingot from Arugal's room in SFK, and Purified Kor Gem via Ulric Frostveil's Seeking the Kor Gem (95042) at Zoram'gar Outpost, Ashenvale.
- [ ] Toxic Soil race caveat: a reported beta data restriction excluded Humans, while direct quest pages say Alliance. Human availability is not live-confirmed by the reviewed sources. Check Alba rather than silently assuming every Alliance race can take it.

### Quest checklist

| Done | Quest | Faction / class | Min. acceptance | Pickup | Prerequisite status | Prerequisite chain & notes | Evidence |

<a id="shadowfang-keep"></a>

## Shadowfang Keep

### Before you travel

- [ ] Warlocks of BOTH factions, level20: pick up The Orb of Soran'ruk1740 directly from Doan Karhan at his camp near Camp Taurajo in the southern Barrens (about49.2,57.2). No prior class quest is documented as required. You need BOTH dungeons: 1 Large Soran'ruk Fragment from Shadowfang Darksouls in SFK and 3 Soran'ruk Fragments from Twilight Acolytes in BFD. Accept before either run; turn in to Doan.
- [ ] Alliance: no non-class faction quest is documented, but Warlock, Rogue and Paladin quests DO exist; do not read 'Horde-only dungeon quests' as 'nothing for an Alliance Warlock'.
- [ ] Alliance Paladins, level 20: obtain The Tome of Valor (1649; trainer/book), see Duthorian Rall in Stormwind's Cathedral of Light → The Tome of Valor (1650) to Daphne Stilwell at the southern end of Longshore, Westfall → defend Daphne (1651) → report to Duthorian (1652) → The Test of Righteousness (1653) to Jordan Stilwell outside Ironforge, Dun Morogh → accept material quest 1654. The four materials are Whitestone Oak Lumber (Deadmines Goblin Woodcarvers), Jordan's Smithing Hammer (SFK stables), Jordan's Refined Ore Shipment (Bailor's Ore Shipment 1655, Bailor Stonehand in Thelsamar, Loch Modan; retrieve ore at Mo'grosh Stronghold), and Purified Kor Gem (Seeking the Kor Gem 1442).
- [ ] Horde Paladins: start Diplomatic Incident (91858; level 18) at Danitha Morr, Bandarion Keep in western Tirisfal Glades → A Curious Pair (91859; Trevan Rol, Sepulcher crypt, Silverpine) → A Grim Fate (91860; Deathguard Baldren, Sepulcher) → Into Fenris Keep (91861; Charred Remains on Fenris Isle) → Lumina Windsinger (91862; unlock her cage in Fenris Keep cellar) → escort The Windshaper's Wrath (96204) → The Debt (95034; Lumina back at the Sepulcher) → A Moon-Kissed Blade (95036; level 20, Lumina gives it, Trevan supplies Weapon Notes and receives materials). Collect Whitestone Oak Lumber in Deadmines, Enchanted Silver Ingot from Arugal's room in SFK, and Purified Kor Gem via Ulric Frostveil's Seeking the Kor Gem (95042) at Zoram'gar Outpost, Ashenvale.
- [ ] Rogues of both factions: practical trigger is the mailed 'An Offer' from C at level20 while in a capital, then Dead Drop in the cabin by Pyrewood Village at the foot of the SFK ramp, Silverpine (about47.1,71.1). The indexed database minimum1 does NOT mean a level1 character can receive the job. The Horn of Xelthos is SOLO: pickpocket Gefell and Gemela, combine half-keys, open Ornamented Chest in the stables. They appear for a solo Rogue, not your normal dungeon group.
- [ ] Horde: pick up Deathstalkers in Shadowfang from High Executor Hadrec and Arugal Must Die from Dalar Dawnweaver at the Sepulcher, Silverpine; The Book of Ur from Keeper Bel'dugur in Undercity's Apothecarium.

<a id="blackfathom-deeps"></a>

## Blackfathom Deeps

### Before you travel

- [ ] Warlocks of BOTH factions, level20: pick up The Orb of Soran'ruk1740 directly from Doan Karhan at his camp near Camp Taurajo in the southern Barrens (about49.2,57.2). No prior class quest is documented as required. You need BOTH dungeons: 1 Large Soran'ruk Fragment from Shadowfang Darksouls in SFK and 3 Soran'ruk Fragments from Twilight Acolytes in BFD. Accept before either run; turn in to Doan.
- [ ] Alliance: get Knowledge in the Deeps from Gerrig Bonegrip in Ironforge's Forlorn Cavern (older summaries mislabel Hall of Explorers). Darnassus, Craftsmen's Terrace: Dawnwatcher Shaedlass gives In Search of Thaelrid1198, needed before Alliance Blackfathom Villainy1200 per current reviewed guides; Argent Guard Manados gives Twilight Falls1199. Turn in 1198 to Thaelrid in the side cave by the first turtle area before continuing to Kelris.
- [ ] Alliance: get Researching the Corruption1275 DIRECTLY from Gershala Nightwhisper in Auberdine, Darkshore. The Corruption Abroad3765 from Argos Nightwhisper in Stormwind's Park is an OPTIONAL breadcrumb to her, not required. Do not make a needless Stormwind detour.
- [ ] Horde: Je'neu Sancrea at Zoram'gar Outpost, Ashenvale, gives The Essence of Aku'Mai6563 and Amongst the Ruins6921 directly. Trouble in the Deeps6562 from Tsunaman, Sun Rock Retreat, Stonetalon Mountains, is an optional breadcrumb. Before entering, farm a Damp Note from Blackfathom Tide Priestesses near the entrance → Allegiance to the Old Gods6564 → return to Je'neu → take kill step6565; otherwise you may need a second trip for Lorgus Jett.
- [ ] Alliance Paladins, level 20: obtain The Tome of Valor (1649; trainer/book), see Duthorian Rall in Stormwind's Cathedral of Light → The Tome of Valor (1650) to Daphne Stilwell at the southern end of Longshore, Westfall → defend Daphne (1651) → report to Duthorian (1652) → The Test of Righteousness (1653) to Jordan Stilwell outside Ironforge, Dun Morogh → accept material quest 1654. The four materials are Whitestone Oak Lumber (Deadmines Goblin Woodcarvers), Jordan's Smithing Hammer (SFK stables), Jordan's Refined Ore Shipment (Bailor's Ore Shipment 1655, Bailor Stonehand in Thelsamar, Loch Modan; retrieve ore at Mo'grosh Stronghold), and Purified Kor Gem (Seeking the Kor Gem 1442). For Kor Gem1442, use Thundris Windweaver in Auberdine, Darkshore, per the direct Forever record; the Paladin guide's Tandaan Lightmane/Astranaar location conflicts with that record.
- [ ] Horde Paladins: start Diplomatic Incident (91858; level 18) at Danitha Morr, Bandarion Keep in western Tirisfal Glades → A Curious Pair (91859; Trevan Rol, Sepulcher crypt, Silverpine) → A Grim Fate (91860; Deathguard Baldren, Sepulcher) → Into Fenris Keep (91861; Charred Remains on Fenris Isle) → Lumina Windsinger (91862; unlock her cage in Fenris Keep cellar) → escort The Windshaper's Wrath (96204) → The Debt (95034; Lumina back at the Sepulcher) → A Moon-Kissed Blade (95036; level 20, Lumina gives it, Trevan supplies Weapon Notes and receives materials). Collect Whitestone Oak Lumber in Deadmines, Enchanted Silver Ingot from Arugal's room in SFK, and Purified Kor Gem via Ulric Frostveil's Seeking the Kor Gem (95042) at Zoram'gar Outpost, Ashenvale. Take Seeking the Kor Gem95042 from Ulric Frostveil at Zoram'gar BEFORE farming naga. Either faction's corrupted gem can drop in the outer entrance caves, so the Paladin side quest need not require an instanced BFD clear.
- [ ] Horde: pick up Blackfathom Villainy6561 directly from Argent Guard Thaelrid inside. Amongst the Ruins involves the Fathom Stone in Moonshrine Ruins and summons Baron Aquanis, whose globe starts Baron Aquanis6922.
- [ ] Database-only caveat: The Heart of the Void78916/78917 appears in additional Forever databases and the ByteTavern summary, but is absent from the main index and Mmonster/WOWF.IO quest lists. Included below as availability UNKNOWN, not a guaranteed live quest or a required pre-trip errand.

<a id="the-stockade"></a>

## The Stockade

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance — WETLANDS FIRST: Motley Garmason, Dun Modr (49.7,18.3): complete The Dark Iron War (#303; requires level25), then accept The Fury Runs Deep. This is the Kam Deepfury quest people miss.
- [ ] Alliance — Deadmines first for The Stockade Riots: loot An Unsent Letter from VanCleef → Baros Alexston, Cathedral Square, Stormwind → Bazil Thredd delivery to Warden Thelwater → accept The Stockade Riots before the kill.
- [ ] Alliance — Lakeshire, Redridge: Guard Berton (What Comes Around...); Darkshire town hall, Duskwood: Councilman Millstipe (Crime and Punishment).
- [ ] Alliance — Stormwind: Warden Thelwater at the prison door (Quell the Uprising); Nikova Raskol patrolling Old Town (The Color of Blood). Horde has no Stockade quests documented.

<a id="excavation-site"></a>

## Excavation Site: Wetlands

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance — Menethil Harbor: James Halloran, complete Young Crocolisk Skins (#484) to unlock Highland Hides; Caitlin Grassman, take Lost in the Thicket Things (Seeking Caitlin from Astranaar is optional).
- [ ] Alliance — Rethiel the Greenwarden, central Wetlands: Tramping Paws → Fire Taboo → Blisters on The Land → Horrors in the Highland. Finish these outdoor quests BEFORE the run.
- [ ] Alliance — Dorin Songblade, Lakeshire armorer, Redridge: take Songblade Search before leaving. It unlocks Fallen in the Fen at Daewyn's body inside.
- [ ] Horde — Borstan in The Drag, Orgrimmar (Changing Tastes); Zaruk in Hammerfall, Arathi (Dragonmaw Rumors) → Deathstalker Agent above Wetlands Dragonmaw camp (Open the Maw).
- [ ] Both factions — Titan Relic is a final-boss drop, not a pre-run pickup. Alliance turns it in to Prospector Whelgar; Horde to Bashana Runetotem, Thunder Bluff; both have post-run delivery followups.

<a id="city-of-dalaran"></a>

## City of Dalaran

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance — SOUTHSHORE FIRST: from level30, Emissary Jacques in Southshore town hall (48.3,60.1): take An Alarming Request and turn it in to the Image of Archmage Modera outside Dalaran. Then take BOTH Heart of Disruption and Starving Arcane before entering. Do not rely on guides calling the Southshore step optional.
- [ ] Alliance — Stormwind before travelling: Shylamiir in The Park herb shop (A Green Sample); High Sorcerer Andromath in the Wizard's Sanctum, Mage Quarter (Power Overwhelming).
- [ ] Horde — Tarren Mill, Magus Wordeen Voidglare: complete BOTH Prison Break In and Key to the City → Dalaran Patrols → Blood in the Streets → Modera: Heart of Disruption. Keeper Bel'varil's quests are unrelated.
- [ ] Horde — Tarren Mill: Melisara (The Grave Knight); Undercity Apothecarium: Doctor Martin Felben (Source of Power); near Dalaran sewer entrance: Rexxie Copperclutch (Opportunistic Education).
- [ ] Entrance key is not the same as quest eligibility: one Dalaran Sewer Key opens the door for the whole group; each character still needs their own prerequisites and quest log entries.

<a id="gnomeregan"></a>

## Gnomeregan

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance — Ironforge Tinker Town: Tinkmaster Overspark (Save Techbot's Brain!), Klockmort Spannerspan (Essential Artificials), Master Mechanic Castpipe (Data Rescue), High Tinker Mekkatorque (The Grand Betrayal).
- [ ] Alliance — Ozzie Togglevolt, Kharanos: finish Gnogaine, return to Ozzie, and accept The Only Cure is More Green Glow BEFORE your main clear. Gnoarn in Ironforge provides The Day After referral.
- [ ] Alliance — Shoni the Shilent, Stormwind Dwarven District: Gyrodrillmatic Excavationators. Darnassus/Stonetalon/Stormwind referral breadcrumbs are listed separately from actual mandatory collection steps.
- [ ] Horde — Nogg, Orgrimmar: accept Rig Wars and keep it in your log → Sovik: Chief Engineer Scooty → Scooty, Booty Bay: Gnomer-gooooone! for the teleporter.
- [ ] Both — Do not skip uninstanced Train Depot: Techbot and punch-card terminal A are outside the portal. Inside: take Kernobee escort, loot Grime-Encrusted Ring, use Sparklematic; ring upgrades happen after the run.

### Quest checklist

<a id="razorfen-kraul"></a>

## Razorfen Kraul

### Before you travel

- [ ] Both — Mebok Mizzyrix, Ratchet: Blueleaf Tubers. ALSO collect the crate, stick and manual beside him; the quest alone is insufficient.
- [ ] Alliance — Loot Lonebrow's Journal from Henrig Lonebrow's corpse at the foot of the Great Lift, Thousand Needles → Falfindel Waywarder, Thalanaar, Feralas: The Crone of the Kraul.
- [ ] Horde — Auld Stonespire, Thunder Bluff (A Vengeful Fate); Master Apothecary Faranell, Undercity Apothecarium (Going, Going, Guano!).
- [ ] Class-only — Warriors prepare Fire Hardened Mail (Alliance, Furen Longbeard in Stormwind via Yorus Barleybrew's Redridge chain) or Brutal Armor (Horde, Thun'grim Firegaze in the Barrens) BEFORE killing Roogug.
- [ ] Class-only — Alliance shaman: Eldrun Stormbreaker, Ironforge, Call of Air → Veyric Thunderhame, Arathi, Elemental Aid. Horde shaman: finish Call of Air → Prate Cloudseer, Thousand Needles, Elemental Aid (not Rau as the starter).
- [ ] Inside — Willix escort for both, Heralath Fallowbrook for Alliance; Horde should loot Charlga's Small Scroll to start the Razorfen Downs preparation chain.

<a id="scarlet-monastery-graveyard"></a>

## Scarlet Monastery — Graveyard

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: Brother Anton (6141), Brother Crowley, Cathedral of Light, Stormwind → Brother Anton at Nijel’s Point, Desolace → Down the Scarlet Path (261): kill 30 Undead Ravagers in Valley of Bones, Desolace → Down the Scarlet Path (1052): carry letter to Raleigh at Southshore inn, Hillsbrad → In the Name of the Light (1053). Guides present 6141 as chain start; its mandatory-versus-breadcrumb status is not independently established, so safest route includes it.
- [ ] Alliance: collect Mythology of the Titans in Ironforge and Past Due from Magus Olvek, Silver Enclave / Tempered Tomes, Dalaran before a Library run. Neither is a Graveyard objective.
- [ ] Horde: Faranell’s Hearts of Zeal requires finishing Going, Going, Guano! in Razorfen Kraul first; take it in Apothecarium, Undercity.
- [ ] Horde: Varimathras, Undercity for three-wing kill quest; Sage Truthseeker, Thunder Bluff for Library book (not Undead per Classic-derived source). Long Test of Lore starts Dorn Plainstalker, cave northeast of Freewind Post, Thousand Needles; full sequence is in row.
- [ ] Mage only: Tabetha, The Quagmire, Dustwallow → Hidden Secrets → Magus Tirth, Mirage Raceway → Get the Scoop → Rituals of Power. Not applicable to a Warlock.
- [ ] Index associates several Library-only quests and three-wing boss quests with Graveyard; those rows are retained but explicitly marked false positives. Do not plan their completion in Graveyard.

### Quest checklist

<a id="scarlet-monastery-library"></a>

## Scarlet Monastery — Library

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: Brother Anton (6141), Brother Crowley, Cathedral of Light, Stormwind → Brother Anton at Nijel’s Point, Desolace → Down the Scarlet Path (261): kill 30 Undead Ravagers in Valley of Bones, Desolace → Down the Scarlet Path (1052): carry letter to Raleigh at Southshore inn, Hillsbrad → In the Name of the Light (1053). Guides present 6141 as chain start; its mandatory-versus-breadcrumb status is not independently established, so safest route includes it.
- [ ] Alliance: collect Mythology of the Titans in Ironforge and Past Due from Magus Olvek, Silver Enclave / Tempered Tomes, Dalaran before a Library run. Neither is a Graveyard objective.
- [ ] Horde: Faranell’s Hearts of Zeal requires finishing Going, Going, Guano! in Razorfen Kraul first; take it in Apothecarium, Undercity.
- [ ] Horde: Varimathras, Undercity for three-wing kill quest; Sage Truthseeker, Thunder Bluff for Library book (not Undead per Classic-derived source). Long Test of Lore starts Dorn Plainstalker, cave northeast of Freewind Post, Thousand Needles; full sequence is in row.
- [ ] Mage only: Tabetha, The Quagmire, Dustwallow → Hidden Secrets → Magus Tirth, Mirage Raceway → Get the Scoop → Rituals of Power. Not applicable to a Warlock.
- [ ] Index associates several Library-only quests and three-wing boss quests with Graveyard; those rows are retained but explicitly marked false positives. Do not plan their completion in Graveyard.

<a id="the-drowned-city"></a>

## The Drowned City

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance — do not sail here without Southshore prework: At Rebel Camp, northern Stranglethorn Vale: Sergeant Yohwa — complete The Second Rebellion (203) and Bad Medicine (204) → Brother Nimetz at same camp: Kurzen’s Mystery (207) → Troll Witchery (205) → An Acquaintance in Southshore (95786), Nimetz sends you with Savage Coast Report Bundle to Loremaster Dibbs, Southshore, Hillsbrad → accept both dungeon quests from Dibbs.
- [ ] Alliance: Ancient Gurubashi Secrets from Archmage Timear, Dalaran, Alterac Mountains.
- [ ] Both: Krazek, Booty Bay for Shady Saltseers and The Saltseer Captain; no required relationship between them established.
- [ ] Horde: Kin’weelay, Grom’gol Base Camp, Stranglethorn: The Vile Reef (629) → The Gurubashi Legends of Old (95748) → Locating the Lost City (95770) → The Drowned City (95775). For The Only Good Brinescale…, also finish Enemies in the Water at Commander Aggro’gosh in Grom’gol; BOTH that quest and Locating the Lost City are required.
- [ ] Horde: Ancient Gurubashi Idol from Mazu’kon, Skywatcher Plateau, Mulgore.
- [ ] Pickups from Dibbs and Kin’weelay are external despite WOWF.IO dungeon-list classification; its direct Magics page confirms Dibbs. New outdoor followups are listed separately after their dungeon steps.

<a id="razorfen-downs"></a>

## Razorfen Downs

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: Bring the Light from Archbishop Benedictus, Cathedral of Light, Stormwind (min 39).
- [ ] Both: A Host of Evil from Myriam Moonsinger on the hill outside RFD; objectives are exterior mobs.
- [ ] Horde: for An Unholy Alliance, first loot Small Scroll from Charlga Razorflank in RAZORFEN KRAUL, turn it in to Varimathras, Royal Quarter, Undercity, then take 6521 for Ambassador Malcin outside RFD. Also Bring the End from Andrew Brownell, Magic Quarter, Undercity.
- [ ] Inside: all party members finish Scourge of the Downs at Belnistrasz’s prison cell BEFORE anyone starts Extinguishing the Idol escort.

### Quest checklist

<a id="scarlet-monastery-cathedral"></a>

## Scarlet Monastery — Cathedral

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: Brother Anton (6141), Brother Crowley, Cathedral of Light, Stormwind → Brother Anton at Nijel’s Point, Desolace → Down the Scarlet Path (261): kill 30 Undead Ravagers in Valley of Bones, Desolace → Down the Scarlet Path (1052): carry letter to Raleigh at Southshore inn, Hillsbrad → In the Name of the Light (1053). Guides present 6141 as chain start; its mandatory-versus-breadcrumb status is not independently established, so safest route includes it.
- [ ] Alliance: collect Mythology of the Titans in Ironforge and Past Due from Magus Olvek, Silver Enclave / Tempered Tomes, Dalaran before a Library run. Neither is a Graveyard objective.
- [ ] Horde: Faranell’s Hearts of Zeal requires finishing Going, Going, Guano! in Razorfen Kraul first; take it in Apothecarium, Undercity.
- [ ] Horde: Varimathras, Undercity for three-wing kill quest; Sage Truthseeker, Thunder Bluff for Library book (not Undead per Classic-derived source). Long Test of Lore starts Dorn Plainstalker, cave northeast of Freewind Post, Thousand Needles; full sequence is in row.
- [ ] Mage only: Tabetha, The Quagmire, Dustwallow → Hidden Secrets → Magus Tirth, Mirage Raceway → Get the Scoop → Rituals of Power. Not applicable to a Warlock.
- [ ] Index associates several Library-only quests and three-wing boss quests with Graveyard; those rows are retained but explicitly marked false positives. Do not plan their completion in Graveyard.

### Quest checklist

<a id="kroldok-stronghold"></a>

## Krol’dok Stronghold

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance military unlock: Ashe Amberhall, Farholde Keep, Riverglades: Scraps of Knowledge (94879) → The Western Front (94886), Demar Brant in Farholde → Sergeant Riftan, Bristle Hills: BOTH No Soldier Left Behind (94888) AND Carving a Path (94889) → Bring Me His Head (94890). Questie observed trace links support this route. Icy guide instead roots this in Field Marshal’s Log (94885); that direct database chain leads to Saving Marshal Ryan → Dying Wish → Delivering Closure, so it is NOT established as a substitute for the military chain.
- [ ] Alliance Tome unlock: Ashe Amberhall, Farholde Keep, Riverglades: Scraps of Knowledge (94879) → Curious Trinkets (94881; collect charms in Sunnyglade) → A Small Favor (94907; capture Twilight Thrall at conduit in Twilight’s Shroud) → Tome of Twilight (94984). Intermediate gating from military chain into Curious Trinkets is not established by consulted sources; if unavailable, complete military chain described above. 
- [ ] Alliance: Vengeance of the Highborne from Ranath Nightstride at Dalaran inn, Alterac Mountains. Min level conflict 37 versus 38; 37 listed by WOWF.IO/Zockify.
- [ ] Horde: Krom’rosh, Rog’mar, Riverglades: BOTH A Message in Blood (93786) AND Food for the Taking (93787) → Show Them How It’s Done (94483) → Tran’gul: Encroaching Twilight → Twilight Missives → Digging for Answers → Clubtoe: The Purple Ones (both stages) → Sickening Lights → Twilight No More! → Spread the Word → Tran’gul: Land of Giants → Clugfist: Might Makes Right → Unworthy Champion → Clugfist’s Decree → Krom’rosh: The Wayward Warrior → Da’grosh: BOTH Carving a Path (94745) AND Break Their Chains (94748) → Bring Me His Head (94768). Token unlocks after Bring Me His Head; Eye and Tome require It Is Time, Brother… (95278) afterward.
- [ ] Key chain starts INSIDE: Tunk near blacksmith/War Handler Ashka drops faction moulding → turn in to Alphonse Dumas, Farholde Keep (Alliance) or Kreza Darkthorn, Rog’mar (Horde), Riverglades → Key Ingredients, 12 Iron Bars + 4 Thick Leather.
- [ ] Database-only Krol’dok No More! escort entries have no verified pickup/live availability; Horde description is NYI. Listed as unverified, not guaranteed obtainable.

<a id="uldaman"></a>

## Uldaman

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: earliest tablet root is Crumpled Map, Hammertoe’s Digsite, Badlands; full sequence: A Sign of Hope (720), Crumpled Map at Hammertoe’s Digsite, Badlands → Prospector Ryedol at dwarf camp south of digsite: A Sign of Hope (721) → Hammertoe Grez in Uldaman exterior tunnels: Amulet of Secrets (722) → Prospect of Faith (723), Hammertoe to Ryedol → Prospect of Faith (724), Ryedol to Historian Karnik, Hall of Explorers, Ironforge → Passing Word of a Threat (725) → An Ambassador of Evil (762; Badlands) → Advisor Belgrum in Hall of Explorers, Ironforge: The Lost Tablets of Will (1139).
- [ ] Alliance: Agmond’s Fate begins Prospector Stormpike, Hall of Explorers, Ironforge → Ironband Wants You! → Find Agmond → Murdaloc → Prospector Ironband, Ironband’s Excavation Site, Loch Modan.
- [ ] Alliance necklace: farm Shattered Necklace from Shadowforge dwarves in exterior tunnels; visit Talvash del Kissel, Mystic Ward, Ironforge → Lore for a Price (bring 5 Silver Bars) → Back to Uldaman. Keep Phial of Scrying to advance inside before killing Archaedas; Replacement Phial is only optional lost-item recovery.
- [ ] Alliance: The Lost Dwarves and Reclaimed Treasures in Hall of Explorers, Ironforge; Badlands Reagent Run from Ghak Healtouch, Thelsamar, Loch Modan must be completed before Uldaman Reagent Run.
- [ ] Horde: start Necklace Recovery with Dran Droffers, The Drag, Orgrimmar; first interior visit to paladin remains sends you out to Jarkal Mossmeld, Kargath, Badlands before gem/power-source stage. Finish Jarkal’s Badlands Reagent Run for Uldaman Reagent Run; Patrick Garrett, Trade Quarter, Undercity for Reclaimed Treasures.
- [ ] Both: Rigglefuzz (central Badlands), Power Stones; Theldurin the Lost (southern Badlands), Solution to Doom. Several objectives are outside instance. Discs start inside after Archaedas.
- [ ] Mage only: city trainer → Return to the Marsh → Tabetha, The Quagmire, Dustwallow → The Infernal Orb → The Exorcism → Power in Uldaman. Not a Warlock quest.

<a id="zulfarrak"></a>

## Zul’Farrak

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: start Nekrum’s Medallion chain with Gryphon Master Talonaxe, Aerie Peak, The Hinterlands: Witherbark Cages → The Altar of Zul → Thadius Grimshade → Nethergarde Keep, Blasted Lands → Nekrum’s Medallion.
- [ ] Mallet preparation for Gahz’rilla: Qiaga the Keeper at Altar of Zul, The Hinterlands drops Sacred Mallet → use at top altar of Jintha’Alor, The Hinterlands → Mallet of Zul’Farrak. Have ONE party member bring it to dungeon gong. This is not a mandatory quest-acceptance prerequisite and is Classic-derived, not live-verified Forever.
- [ ] Both: Yeh’kinya, Steamwheedle Port, Tanaris → Screecher Spirits (go Feralas) → return for The Prophecy of Mosh’aru; this later continues into Sunken Temple/LBRS.
- [ ] Both: collect Gadgetzan quests from Tran’rek, Trenton Lighthammer and Chief Engineer Bilgewhizzle. Tiara from Tabetha, The Quagmire, Dustwallow; Gahz’rilla from Wizzle Brassbolts, Mirage Raceway, Thousand Needles. Tran’rek, Tabetha’s Task, The Brassbolts Brothers are OPTIONAL breadcrumbs, not mandatory chains.
- [ ] Horde: Venom Bottle at Witherbark troll camps, The Hinterlands → Venom Bottles → Apothecary Lydon, Tarren Mill, Hillsbrad (direct Forever quest confirms; guide’s Alterac placement is wrong) → Undamaged Venom Sac → Consult Master Gadrin → Sen’jin Village, Durotar → The Spider God.
- [ ] Zul’Farrak source explicitly says Classic-derived because dungeon was unavailable in Forever beta; mallet and original chain details are not presented as live-verified.

<a id="maraudon"></a>

## Maraudon

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: collect Shadowshard Fragments from Archmage Tervosh in Theramore (Dustwallow), then Talendria and Keeper Marandis at Nijel’s Point, Desolace. Horde equivalents: Uthel’nay in Orgrimmar, Vark Battlescar in Shadowprey Village and Selendra on the coast south of it.
- [ ] NEW Forever quest prework: Strange Pylons (92914), Shen’dralar Pylon object in Shen’dralas -&gt; Mordent Evenshade at Shen’dralas -&gt; Fragments of the Pylons (96010;Magram/Gelkis villages) -&gt; Powering the Pylons (96018;Mana Sieve, restore pylons) -&gt; A Brief History Lesson (93016) -&gt; Meeting the Magister (93394;Mordent sends to Magister Kirandis in temple) -&gt; Forlorn Gardens (93341), Mithera at nearby Shen’dralas temple -&gt; Word of the Woods (93594), Mithera sends to Woodwyrd at Forlorn Gardens -&gt; Rot and Regret (93404). Do not assume Woodwyrd will offer Rot and Regret on first arrival. Horde also visits Olana Brighthoof at Camp Gev’rek, Mulgore for An’she’s Radiance (earlier prerequisites unresolved).
- [ ] Both: visit Willow at Kormek’s Hut, Desolace, the wandering Centaur Pariah in southern Desolace, and Cavindra in Maraudon’s orange OUTER caves. These are separate pickups, not a mandatory shared chain.
- [ ] Plan a full orange+purple route, not a Princess teleport: fill the Vyletongue vial in the outer orange pool, do Shadowshard Fragments in outer purple caves, collect all five khan gems for the Pariah, and start Legends before killing Noxxion/Vyletongue. After Celebras, take The Scepter of Celebras and stay for the ritual.
- [ ] After Princess Theradras, talk to Zaetar’s Spirit for Seed of Life BEFORE leaving; its delivery is in Moonglade. Classic-derived chains below are not individually live-verified.

<a id="alcaz-prison"></a>

## Alcaz Prison

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: Our Man in Theramore (95194) — Master Mathias Shaw, Stormwind City (SI:7) -&gt; Sully McCleary on Theramore Isle, Dustwallow Marsh -&gt; Watch Yer Back (95196;meet Sully in Dustwallow) -&gt; The Return of the King? (95198;use SI:7 Disguise Kit to eavesdrop in Theramore Mage Tower, then return to Sully). THEN accept BOTH The Secret Prison (95262) and A Bit of Wet Work (96381) from Sully before travelling to the island. They are parallel branches. The index’s single-quest Alcaz list omits this entire SI:7 chain.
- [ ] Alliance, separate pickup from level46: The Dragon’s Journal (96772) from Archmage Lan’dalock in Dalaran, Alterac Mountains. Direct WOWF record identifies him (older guides said giver unknown). Earlier required chain remains unresolved, so check before grouping rather than assuming he has no gate.
- [ ] Horde: The Missing Kor’kron (95283) — Eitrigg, Grommash Hold, Valley of Wisdom, Orgrimmar -&gt; Nazeer Bloodpike, Brackenwall Village, Dustwallow Marsh -&gt; The Elite of the Elite (95284;find Sergeant Ulka on Alcaz Island) -&gt; Right Place, Wrong Time (95285;talk to Ulka outside prison). THEN take No Orc Left Behind, A Little Payback and Blood Red Bandanas together at Ulka. Do not follow a database’s flattened list as though the two side quests require each other.
- [ ] Inside: Horde speaks to Folkar for The Honor of the Kor’kron, inspects the king-search evidence/Perfumed Note after Blazeroar, returns to Folkar for Bad News and Thrall Needs to Know BEFORE leaving. Alliance checks the same king-search route for Somber News, then returns to Sully and ultimately Shaw.
- [ ] Both: speak to Mason Connery INSIDE for The Man in the Iron Chains before passing The Questioner; recover the shackle key, return to Mason and take More Valuable Than Gold before leaving. That continuation is a Westfall treasure hunt, followed by faction-specific State Secrets (Document Box). Older report separately mentions an unnamed Zarla Ober shackle quest; its exact identity/ID is unresolved, not invented here.
- [ ] Published sources conflict: Mmonster November10 lists only96772; older ByteTavern says no quests known; WOWF’s individual records plus observed Questie relation traces document the expanded chains here. Traces label their links as inferences from character observations, not authoritative server scripts. Exact new-NPC coordinates are deliberately not guessed.

<a id="sunken-temple"></a>

## Sunken Temple (Temple of Atal’Hakkar)

### Before you travel

- [ ] WARLOCK, level50: at your major-city warlock trainer take An Imp’s Request (8419), bring1 Felcloth to Impsy just north of Bloodvenom Falls, Felwood, then finish The Wrong Stuff (8421:10 Rotting Wood +4 Bloodvenom Essence) and ACCEPT Trolls of a Feather (8422) before entering. Hot and Itchy (8420) is an alternative opening at Impsy, NOT an extra sequential step; Hot Fiery Death is unrelated.
- [ ] Alliance entry quest has substantial external prework: In Search of The Temple — Brohann Caskbelly, Dwarven District, Stormwind -&gt; To The Hinterlands (Falstad Wildhammer, Aerie Peak) -&gt; Gryphon Master Talonaxe (Aerie Peak aviary) -&gt; Rhapsody Shindigger (Shindigger’s Camp, Hinterlands) -&gt; Rhapsody’s Kalimdor Kocktail (3 Roc Gizzards from Tanaris, 3 Ironfur Livers and 3 Groddoc Livers from Feralas) -&gt; Rhapsody’s Tale -&gt; return to Brohann for Into The Temple of Atal’Hakkar (1475).
- [ ] Horde entry quest external prework: Pool of Tears — Fel’zerul, Stonard, Swamp of Sorrows (collect underwater artifacts) -&gt; The Atal’ai Exile (Shadra’Alor, Hinterlands) -&gt; Return to Fel’Zerul -&gt; The Temple of Atal’Hakkar (1445).
- [ ] Both, Hakkar: Yeh’kinya at Steamwheedle Port, Tanaris -&gt; Screecher Spirits (Feralas) -&gt; The Prophecy of Mosh’aru (Zul’Farrak) -&gt; The Ancient Egg (Jintha’Alor, Hinterlands) -&gt; The God Hakkar. Do not arrive expecting to start the Avatar without the quest item.
- [ ] Both, statue quests: The Sunken Temple — Angelas Moonbreeze, Feathermoon Stronghold, Feralas (Alliance,3445), or Witch Doctor Uzer’i, Camp Mojache, Feralas (Horde,3380) -&gt; Marvon Rivetseeker, Broken Pillar, Tanaris -&gt; The Stone Circle (3444; fetch Marvon’s Chest in Ratchet, Barrens, then return). Take BOTH Into the Depths and Secret of the Circle before departing Tanaris. Also collect Jammal’an the Prophet from the Atal’ai Exile at Shadra’Alor in the Hinterlands.
- [ ] Atal’ai Haze: Alliance starts Muigin and Larion at Muigin, Marshal’s Refuge, Un’Goro -&gt; A Visit to Gregan -&gt; Gregan Brewspewer near Twin Colossals, Feralas -&gt; Haze of Evil. Horde starts Larion and Muigin at Larion, same refuge -&gt; Marvon’s Workshop -&gt; Liv Rizzlefix, Ratchet -&gt; Zapper Fuel.
- [ ] All other classes: do the FULL trainer-rooted chain in your table row first; none of the class finales can be picked up merely by reaching ST. Druid needs Atal’alarion; hunter/mage/priest/rogue need Morphaz; warlock/warrior/paladin/shaman need all six balcony trolls. Tell the group before route selection. Documented Forever faction gates remain Alliance-paladin/Horde-shaman despite new cross-faction class options.
- [ ] Inside, loot Shade of Eranikus’s Essence and complete its quest at the Essence Font in the SAME room before leaving. Classic-derived chain details are not each live-verified; min-level conflicts are called out in rows.

## Blackrock Depths

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance Windsor ROOT before first BRD: Dragonkin Menace (4182) — Helendis Riverhorn, Morgan’s Vigil, Burning Steppes -&gt; The True Masters (4183;Magistrate Solomon in Lakeshire, Redridge) -&gt; The True Masters (4184;Bolvar Fordragon, Stormwind Keep) -&gt; The True Masters (4185;talk to Lady Katrana Prestor then Bolvar) -&gt; The True Masters (4186;back to Solomon) -&gt; The True Masters (4223;Marshal Maxwell, Morgan’s Vigil) -&gt; The True Masters (4224;hear Ragged John’s story in Burning Steppes, return to Maxwell) -&gt; Marshal Windsor (4241).
- [ ] Alliance Princess ROOT: The Smoldering Ruins of Thaurissan (3702;listen) — Royal Historian Archesonus, High Seat, Ironforge -&gt; same-name3701 (collect Thaurissan Relics in Ruins of Thaurissan, Burning Steppes;return to Archesonus) -&gt; Kharan Mighthammer (4341), King Magni, High Seat, Ironforge. First visit ends with Kharan’s Tale and The Bearer of Bad News; return to Ironforge for The Fate of the Kingdom BEFORE your Emperor run. Spare Moira. This is separate from Windsor and does NOT require The Love Potion.
- [ ] Alliance other stops: Jalinda Sprig/Oralius at Morgan’s Vigil, Burning Steppes; Ragnar Thunderbrew at Kharanos, Dun Morogh (Enohar’s Nethergarde breadcrumb is optional). Kill Pyron OUTSIDE BRD and return to Jalinda for Incendius! before proceeding inside.
- [ ] Horde ROOT at Thunderheart, Kargath, Badlands: Disharmony of Flame -&gt; kill Pyron outside BRD -&gt; return to Kargath for Disharmony of Fire, The Last Element and Commander Gor’shak. Initial Gor’shak/Kharan visit then requires a trip to Thrall in Orgrimmar before returning for The Royal Rescue; spare Moira.
- [ ] Horde other external roots: WANTED poster at Kargath -&gt; two KILL ON SIGHT stages (return between them) -&gt; Lexlort’s Grark Lorkrub -&gt; Precarious Predicament escort -&gt; Goretooth’s Operation: Death to Angerforge. Apothecary Zinge in Undercity gives Vivian Lagrave -&gt; Lost Thunderbrew Recipe in Kargath. Hierophant Theodora in Kargath gives Rise of the Machines4061 -&gt;4062 -&gt; Lotwil’s4063.
- [ ] Both: Yorba Screwspigot at Steamwheedle Port, Tanaris -&gt; Yuka Screwspigot -&gt; Ribbly at Flame Crest, Burning Steppes; collect Heart of the Mountain from Maxwort there. For Bael’Gar, finish ONE of the alternate Taste of Flame routes in its row; the full Searing Gorge torch chain is not universally mandatory.
- [ ] Before resurrecting during a death: speak to Franclorn Forgewright’s ghost in the central Blackrock Mountain tomb, finish3801 and accept3802 for Shadowforge Key. Min55: also take Attunement to the Core from Lothos on the lower chains.
- [ ] RETURN-VISIT WARNINGS: Windsor’s Abandoned Hope MUST be turned in to Maxwell outside before the Note continuation; both factions’ Princess chain requires a capital-city turn-in before Emperor; Love Potion starts INSIDE and sends you to Azshara/Un’Goro before returning INSIDE. Do not promise these all in one uninterrupted clear.
- [ ] Mining230: bring20 Gold Bars,10 Truesilver Bars,2 Star Rubies and ore you want smelted. Speak to Gloom’rel BEFORE activating the Seven; pay Chalice, then speak to Gloom’rel again. Grim Guzzler pickups/events should be done before making the bar hostile.
- [ ] Level58 Set2 quests are included despite questlevel60: The Challenge requires the FULL class/faction upgrade chain in its row and a timed Stratholme run. Three Kings of Flame comes later in that same chain and adds another Incendius kill plus UBRS and Silithus. Their publication in databases is not proof that every step is currently obtainable on the live server.

<a id="dire-maul-east"></a>

## Dire Maul — East

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: take Feathermoon Stronghold (#7494) from Crier Goodman/Stormwind (alternatives Courier Hammerfall/Ironforge or Herald Moonstalker/Darnassus) BEFORE going to Feralas, hand it to Latronicus Moonspear at Feathermoon, then take Lethtendris's Web (#7488). Questie Forever observed prerequisite edge supports treating #7494 as required, not an optional breadcrumb.
- [ ] Both factions: Azj'Tordin at Lariss Pavilion/Feralas for Pusillin and the Elder Azj'Tordin (#7441). Pusillin's Crescent Key drop is independent of this quest; chase/talk to him then kill him if planning West/North/library access.
- [ ] Shards prework: Rabine Saturna, Nighthaven/Moonglade → A Reliquary of Purity (#5527) → Southwind Village/Silithus → return Rabine → Shards of the Felvine (#5526). Carry the provided reliquary before fighting Alzzin.
- [ ] Horde: Camp Mojache (#7492) from Warcaller Gorlach in Orgrimmar, Harbinger Balthazad in Undercity, or Bluff Runner Windstrider in Thunder Bluff → Talo Thornhoof at Camp Mojache/Feralas → Lethtendris's Web (#7489). Source index wrongly concatenates Harbinger with Orgrimmar. Horde prerequisite edge is guide-derived, not independently observed in reviewed traces.
- [ ] Library access: the Athenaeum is shared between West and North. Classic route enters North then uses the Crescent-Key door on the lower level near Guard Mol'dar, avoiding a full West clear; use caution around patrols. Elven Legends/library quests do not require killing Prince merely to reach their NPCs. Crescent Key drops from Pusillin in East without needing Azj'Tordin's quest; it is door access, not a quest-chain prerequisite. West/North door enforcement in Forever is not live-verified; Classic alternatives include a skilled rogue opening the lock.
- [ ] Warlock at54+: Harnessing Shadows (#7502) and all nine matching-class books are in scope, including book drops in East. Books may be traded/bought; take your class book to the library (Warlock: Mykos). These are NOT the level60 Dreadsteed ritual. Arcane Refreshment (#7463, Mage) and The Prison's Bindings (#7581, Warlock) require60 and are excluded.
- [ ] Late Isalien summons, only if Set2 enabled: Dungeon Set 2 root (matching class/faction, min 58): Deliana, Ironforge/The High Seat throne room (Alliance; Warlock An Earnest Proposition #8911), or Mokvar, Orgrimmar/Grommash Hold in the Valley of Wisdom (Horde; Warlock #8919). An Earnest Proposition → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan/Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → matching-class Just Compensation → In Search of Anthion → Dead Man's Plea (timed Stratholme rescue) → Proof of Life → Anthion's Strange Request. Use the Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer for Anthion outside Stratholme and later Bodley; this is NOT the unrelated Scholomance Spectral Essence. → Anthion's Old Friend (#8948; Anthion outside Stratholme, Eastern Plaguelands) → Falrin's Vendetta (#8949; Falrin, Athenaeum/library). → The Instigator's Enchantment (#8950) → The Challenge (#9015; BRD Ring of the Law) → matching-class Anthion's Parting Words (return to Deliana/Mokvar; Warlock #8958 Alliance/#9021 Horde) → Bodley's Unfortunate Fate (#8960 Alliance/#9032 Horde; Bodley by Blackrock Spire portal) → Three Kings of Flame (#8961: BRD Incendius, UBRS Emberseer, Duke of Cynders in Silithus, Hallowed Brazier) → assigned Components of Importance → assigned Left Piece of Lord Valthalak's Amulet. The Components/Left Piece quests are alternatives, not all required in sequence. For a right-piece assignment: Dungeon Set 2 root (matching class/faction, min 58): Deliana, Ironforge/The High Seat throne room (Alliance; Warlock An Earnest Proposition #8911), or Mokvar, Orgrimmar/Grommash Hold in the Valley of Wisdom (Horde; Warlock #8919). An Earnest Proposition → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan/Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → matching-class Just Compensation → In Search of Anthion → Dead Man's Plea (timed Stratholme rescue) → Proof of Life → Anthion's Strange Request. Use the Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer for Anthion outside Stratholme and later Bodley; this is NOT the unrelated Scholomance Spectral Essence. → Anthion's Old Friend (#8948; Anthion outside Stratholme, Eastern Plaguelands) → Falrin's Vendetta (#8949; Falrin, Athenaeum/library). → The Instigator's Enchantment (#8950) → The Challenge (#9015; BRD Ring of the Law) → matching-class Anthion's Parting Words (return to Deliana/Mokvar; Warlock #8958 Alliance/#9021 Horde) → Bodley's Unfortunate Fate (#8960 Alliance/#9032 Horde; Bodley by Blackrock Spire portal) → Three Kings of Flame (#8961: BRD Incendius, UBRS Emberseer, Duke of Cynders in Silithus, Hallowed Brazier) → assigned Components of Importance → assigned Left Piece of Lord Valthalak's Amulet. The Components/Left Piece quests are alternatives, not all required in sequence. → I See Alcaz Island in Your Future... (#8970; Bloodkelp on Alcaz Island, Dustwallow Marsh) → assigned More Components of Importance → assigned Right Piece of Lord Valthalak's Amulet. Left and right assignments may use different dungeons; do not promise both summons on the same visit. These are assigned alternatives, not mandatory every-dungeon sweeps or both pickups before one run.

<a id="blackmaw-hold"></a>

## Blackmaw Hold

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance roots FIRST: Commander Truesong in southern Azshara (DB11.8,78.3; subzone not named) gives Beneath Nordrassil #92345 → Mathrengyl Bearwalker, Cenarion Enclave, Darnassus → Get to the Roots of It #92344. Horde: Jediga at Valormok, Azshara gives #92346 → Nara Wildmane on Elder Rise, Thunder Bluff → #92343. Questie Nov12 handoff traces corroborate required roots; do NOT just fly to the dungeon or directly to the capital expecting the follow-up.
- [ ] Alliance: visit Archmage Timear in Dalaran (Alterac Mountains) for Blackmaw Magic #97173. Horde: Muln Earthfury in northern Mulgore for #97198 (DB also lists Desolace spawn). Both quests need Elder Shaman Bloodmane’s ritual totem. Exact prior unlocks are not established.
- [ ] Visit Ogtinc on the cliffs northeast of Ruins of Eldarath, Azshara (42.4,42.6), for Claws That Rend, Tusks that Gore. Plan BOTH Withermaw and Bonesnout branches. Visit Ekkal at Ruins of Eldarath (42.0,38.9) for Blackmaw Pouches; two indexed-by-newer-DB pouch IDs are retained but second offer/relationship uncertain.
- [ ] Korsa — Azshara, outside Legashi Stronghold: Master Feardred #86777 + Thinning Their Forces #86792, and Later, Satyr #86796 from captive Graal inside the OUTDOOR Legashi Stronghold → Return to Rageroar #86797 (Gatekeeper Rageroar, Ursolan) → Timbermaw Reinforcements #86798 (travel to Gorn One Eye at Timbermaw Hold, Felwood/Winterspring/Moonglade passage) → Chieftain Bloodmaw #87346 (Felpaw Village, Felwood; return to Gorn) → Blackmaw Counter-Offensive #86814 (plant Timbermaw War Banner at Legashi Stronghold summoning circle in Azshara, kill Prince Halanox, report to Rageroar) → Blackmaw Reclamation #92065. Timbermaw Hold reputation gate: older guide says Neutral, Nov13 guide reports Friendly is sufficient; exact lowest accepted standing unresolved. Establish non-hostile access before trying Ursolan givers. Icy’s chain UI says “One of” for the three earliest Legashi tasks; AND/OR relationship unresolved, so doing all three is the safe preparation. This is substantial OUTDOOR work in Azshara AND Felwood before the dungeon head drop.
- [ ] Ursolan is a separate outside stop for Lorekeeper Nok (The Twin Bears) and Gatekeeper Rageroar, not the Blackmaw portal. Watch inside for Feathered Charm Necklace (A Lost Memento, to Unele outside) and hungry lasher exchange Feeding Time! (repeatable; exact NPC/starting gate unresolved).

<a id="lower-blackrock-spire"></a>

## Lower Blackrock Spire

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance first: Helendis Riverhorn at Morgan's Vigil/Burning Steppes for Put Her Down; both factions Kibler and Ragged John at Flame Crest/Burning Steppes for the independent pet quests and Mother's Milk. Bring cages/provided items, and warn the group not to cleanse Mother's Milk.
- [ ] Alliance repeat-run trap: Bijou inside LBRS gives Bijou's Belongings (#5001) → Message to Maxwell (#5002) → leave for Marshal Maxwell at Morgan's Vigil → accept Maxwell's Mission (#5081) → return to LBRS. Separately, loot Wyrmthalak's General Drakkisath's Command (#5089), hand it to Maxwell, and only then take UBRS #5102. Neither chain substitutes for the other.
- [ ] The Final Tablets long prework: Yeh'kinya, Steamwheedle Port/Tanaris: Screecher Spirits (#3520; Feralas) → The Prophecy of Mosh'aru (#3527; Zul'Farrak) → The Ancient Egg (#4787; Jintha'Alor, Hinterlands) → The God Hakkar (#3528; Sunken Temple) → Prospector Ironboot, Steamwheedle Port/Tanaris: The Lost Tablets of Mosh'aru (#5065; Eastern Plaguelands) → The Final Tablets (#4788; LBRS).
- [ ] Horde external pickups: Lexlort (Operative Bijou), Galamav the Marksman (The Pack Mistress), and Warlord Goretooth (Blood and Thunder/Command dialogue) in Kargath/Badlands. Warlord Goretooth, Kargath/Badlands: Blood and Thunder (named by Blizzard, ID not established) → Warlord's Command (#4903; item Warlord Goretooth's Command, LBRS Omokk/Voone/Wyrmthalak plus documents) → Eitrigg's Wisdom (#4941; Eitrigg then Thrall, Grommash Hold/Valley of Wisdom, Orgrimmar) → For The Horde! (#4974; Rend).
- [ ] Entry/access is separate from quest prerequisites: LBRS itself does not require Seal of Ascension. For UBRS, plan a group keyholder; the two Seal quests start with Vaelan inside LBRS, then require Emberstrife forging in Dustwallow and return to Vaelan. Forever's upper-door enforcement is not independently confirmed. Drakefire Amulet is for Onyxia's Lair, NOT for entering UBRS.
- [ ] Weaponsmiths: Kilram in Everlook/Winterspring gives Snakestone of the Shadow Huntress (#5306), requiring Blacksmithing250 +Weaponsmith. Hot Fiery Death is an inside Human Remains pickup, not pre-travel work; its later smithing chain is not a generic Warlock quest.
- [ ] Late Set2 prework, only if enabled: Dungeon Set 2 root (matching class/faction, min 58): Deliana, Ironforge/The High Seat throne room (Alliance; Warlock An Earnest Proposition #8911), or Mokvar, Orgrimmar/Grommash Hold in the Valley of Wisdom (Horde; Warlock #8919). An Earnest Proposition → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan/Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → matching-class Just Compensation → In Search of Anthion → Dead Man's Plea (timed Stratholme rescue) → Proof of Life → Anthion's Strange Request. Use the Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer for Anthion outside Stratholme and later Bodley; this is NOT the unrelated Scholomance Spectral Essence. → Anthion's Old Friend (#8948; Anthion outside Stratholme, Eastern Plaguelands) → Falrin's Vendetta (#8949; Falrin, Athenaeum/library). → The Instigator's Enchantment (#8950) → The Challenge (#9015; BRD Ring of the Law) → matching-class Anthion's Parting Words (return to Deliana/Mokvar; Warlock #8958 Alliance/#9021 Horde) → Bodley's Unfortunate Fate (#8960 Alliance/#9032 Horde; Bodley by Blackrock Spire portal) → Three Kings of Flame (#8961: BRD Incendius, UBRS Emberseer, Duke of Cynders in Silithus, Hallowed Brazier) → assigned Components of Importance → assigned Left Piece of Lord Valthalak's Amulet. The Components/Left Piece quests are alternatives, not all required in sequence. For a right-piece assignment continue through I See Alcaz Island in Your Future... and the assigned More Components of Importance before returning to the assigned dungeon. The full route is in each amulet row; use Ghost Revealer, not Spectral Essence.
- [ ] Falrin's Vendetta is an optional alternate dungeon objective here: accept from Falrin in the Dire Maul library first (full Set2 chain in row), then collect Spirestone Ogre Warbeads in LBRS OR Gordok Warbeads in Dire Maul.

<a id="dire-maul-north"></a>

## Dire Maul — North

### Before you travel

- [ ] Library access: the Athenaeum is shared between West and North. Classic route enters North then uses the Crescent-Key door on the lower level near Guard Mol'dar, avoiding a full West clear; use caution around patrols. Elven Legends/library quests do not require killing Prince merely to reach their NPCs. Crescent Key drops from Pusillin in East without needing Azj'Tordin's quest; it is door access, not a quest-chain prerequisite. West/North door enforcement in Forever is not live-verified; Classic alternatives include a skilled rogue opening the lock.
- [ ] Tribute preparation: bring 1 Frost Oil +1 Thorium Widget, and for Knot's suit 4 Bolts of Runecloth +8 Rugged Leather +2 Rune Thread; Ogre Tannin is inside. Alternative: a pre-crafted/traded suit, or Tailoring/Leatherworking crafting (Classic275) using 2 Bolts +4 Leather +1 Thread +1 Tannin. Make the suit BEFORE freeing Knot. Suit is optional if not doing the disguise route; a normal boss clear does not require it.
- [ ] For Tribute, spare Kreeg and Kromcrush; after King Gordok, speak to Mizzle the Crafty for King of the Gordok, return to the friendly NPCs for The Gordok Taste Test and Unfinished Gordok Business. The latter also has a suit-disguise pickup alternative: no need to claim a full Tribute clear is the only path.
- [ ] A normal boss clear can use Mol'dar's Inner Door Key. A Tribute route needs an alternative door opener (Classic300 Lockpicking, Powerful Seaforium Charge, or Truesilver Skeleton Key) to leave Mol'dar alive; this is access planning, not a prerequisite quest. Loot Fengus's Chest for the Courtyard Key.
- [ ] Free Knot! initial #5525 and repeatable #7429 need Gordok Shackle Key. Craft/make all needed suits first, then free him; do not burn your run's suit service. Indexed Free Knot/Taste Test min56 conflicts with WOWF57; both remain &lt;=59 but57 is the safer planning threshold.
- [ ] Warlock at54+: North can supply Harnessing Shadows (#7502); turn in at Mykos in library. All nine class books and three Librams are repeated here because the library is accessible from North and WOWF maps them here. Librams require completed Elven Legends from your Feralas faction hub; see West for the full external pickup-and-return loop.
- [ ] Before collecting Falrin's Ogre Warbeads, accept Falrin's Vendetta in the library after the full Set2 chain: Dungeon Set 2 root (matching class/faction, min 58): Deliana, Ironforge/The High Seat throne room (Alliance; Warlock An Earnest Proposition #8911), or Mokvar, Orgrimmar/Grommash Hold in the Valley of Wisdom (Horde; Warlock #8919). An Earnest Proposition → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan/Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → matching-class Just Compensation → In Search of Anthion → Dead Man's Plea (timed Stratholme rescue) → Proof of Life → Anthion's Strange Request. Use the Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer for Anthion outside Stratholme and later Bodley; this is NOT the unrelated Scholomance Spectral Essence. → Anthion's Old Friend (#8948; Anthion outside Stratholme, Eastern Plaguelands) → Falrin's Vendetta (#8949; Falrin, Athenaeum/library). The ogres may instead be killed in LBRS; no need for both dungeons. Late Set2 availability in Forever is not live-verified.
- [ ] Unfinished Gordok Business sends you North → West Prince/chest → North. Source IDs #7703/#1318 duplicate the name/objective; neither is claimed a second separate completion.

<a id="dire-maul-west"></a>

## Dire Maul — West

### Before you travel

- [ ] Library access: the Athenaeum is shared between West and North. Classic route enters North then uses the Crescent-Key door on the lower level near Guard Mol'dar, avoiding a full West clear; use caution around patrols. Elven Legends/library quests do not require killing Prince merely to reach their NPCs. Crescent Key drops from Pusillin in East without needing Azj'Tordin's quest; it is door access, not a quest-chain prerequisite. West/North door enforcement in Forever is not live-verified; Classic alternatives include a skilled rogue opening the lock.
- [ ] Alliance ordinary pickup: Scholar Runethorn at Feathermoon Stronghold/Feralas for Elven Legends (#7482); Horde: Sage Korolusk at Camp Mojache/Feralas for #7481. Inspect Kariel Winthalus's remains in library, RETURN to Feralas to hand it in, then make another library visit for Libram turn-ins. Class books do not have this documented prerequisite.
- [ ] Warlock at54+: bring Harnessing Shadows (#7502) to Lorekeeper Mykos in the library. All nine class books below are available only to their matching class; all are retained instead of silently omitting non-Warlock quests. No West boss clear is required solely to return a book if using North's library access.
- [ ] Pick The Madness Within (#7461) from the Shen'dralar Ancient on the Court of the Highborne bridge BEFORE killing Immol'thar and Prince. After both kills return Ancient for The Treasure of the Shen'dralar (#7877), then backtrack to library chest.
- [ ] Unfinished Gordok Business prework is in NORTH, not West: use suit disguise OR become King of the Gordok while leaving Kromcrush alive; accept #7703 (source also has #1318) from him before going West for The Prince's Chest. Then return North. Suit quest itself is not mandatory if using the King route.
- [ ] Librams are repeatable item/reagent turn-ins after Elven Legends, not profession quests. Indexed min54 conflicts with WOWF58/ClassicDB57: still &lt;=59, but do not promise pickup at54. Enchanted Thorium books are a separate Blacksmithing300/item gate with DISPUTED min50 vs WOWF60; retained as uncertain indexed rows, not promised &lt;=59 content.
- [ ] Set2 prework before Falrin/library stages, only if enabled: Dungeon Set 2 root (matching class/faction, min 58): Deliana, Ironforge/The High Seat throne room (Alliance; Warlock An Earnest Proposition #8911), or Mokvar, Orgrimmar/Grommash Hold in the Valley of Wisdom (Horde; Warlock #8919). An Earnest Proposition → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan/Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → matching-class Just Compensation → In Search of Anthion → Dead Man's Plea (timed Stratholme rescue) → Proof of Life → Anthion's Strange Request. Use the Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer for Anthion outside Stratholme and later Bodley; this is NOT the unrelated Scholomance Spectral Essence. → Anthion's Old Friend (#8948) → Falrin's Vendetta (#8949; ogres in North or LBRS) → The Instigator's Enchantment (#8950; ghosts in West) → The Challenge (#9015; library pickup, BRD objective). Expect library revisits between steps.
- [ ] Explicit level60 exclusions: Arcane Refreshment (#7463), Dreadsteed of Xoroth (#7631), Ancient Equine Spirit (#7643), Blessed Arcanite Barding (#7644), Foror's Compendium (#7507) and both Forging of Quel'Serrar (#7508/#7509). Quel'Serrar additionally needs Onyxia, whose raid is not open until December9; do not present as presently completable. The Prison's Bindings (#7581) is also60 and actually has an EAST satyr objective despite WOWF's West mapping.

<a id="scholomance"></a>

## Scholomance

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: begin the Skeleton Key chain with Commander Ashlam Valorfist at Chillwind Camp, Western Plaguelands: Clear the Way #5092 → All Along the Watchtowers #5097 → Scholomance #5533 → Skeletal Fragments #5537 (Alchemist Arbington, same camp) → Mold Rhymes With... #5538 → Fire Plume Forged #5801 (Krinkle Goodsteel, Gadgetzan, Tanaris;15 gold and2 Thorium Bars; forge atop Fire Plume Ridge, Un’Goro) → Araj’s Scarab #5803 (Andorhal) → The Key to Scholomance #5505. Horde equivalent starts Scarlet Diversions #5096 with Derrington at the Bulwark, Tirisfal, then #5098 → #838 → #964 → #5514 → #5802 → #5804 → #5511 with Dithers. Capital A Call to Arms quests are OPTIONAL breadcrumbs. Forever door requirement not confirmed by cited launch guide; arrange a keyholder rather than assuming unlocked.
- [ ] Take Barov Family Fortune from Weldon Barov at Chillwind Camp (Alliance) / Alexi Barov at the Bulwark (Horde), and Doctor Theolen Krastinov, the Butcher from Eva Sarkhoff outside Scholomance at Caer Darrow. Barov deeds are INSIDE Scholo, not in the towns named on them. Take Plagued Hatchlings from Betina in Eastern Plaguelands; current WOWF.IO says Eastwall Tower, old guides Light’s Hope (location conflict).
- [ ] Plan Eva’s sequential outside hand-ins: Doctor #5382 → Bag #5515 → Kirtonos #5384. Save Jandice until Bag is accepted; carry Blood of Innocents for Kirtonos. Ordinary runs may require several lockouts; a patient group can backtrack outside between steps. Healthy Dragon Scale only follows Plagued Hatchlings turn-in, commonly requiring another visit.
- [ ] Start with Eva Sarkhoff — Western Plaguelands, Caer Darrow, outside Scholomance: Doctor Theolen Krastinov, the Butcher #5382 → Krastinov’s Bag of Horrors #5515 → Kirtonos the Herald #5384 → equip Spectral Essence → The Human, Ras Frostwhisper #5461 (Magistrate Marduke, Caer Darrow; retrieve Keepsake of Remembrance from ruined buildings in Stromgarde Keep, Arathi Highlands) → The Dying, Ras Frostwhisper #5462 (Marduke sends you to Leonid in Eastern Plaguelands) → Menethil’s Gift #5463 (Leonid; unholy ground by Baron Rivendare, Stratholme Undead) → Menethil’s Gift #5464 (ground-object return quest) → Soulbound Keepsake #5465 (Leonid → Marduke). Only THEN accept The Lich, Ras Frostwhisper #5466; use Soulbound Keepsake to make Ras HUMAN before killing him. This chain cannot be collected in full before a first Scholo run.
- [ ] Tinkee Steamboil — Burning Steppes, Flame Crest: Broodling Essence → Felnok Steelspring (Everlook, Winterspring) → Chillwind Horns → Return to Tinkee → Egg Freezing #4734 (UBRS Rookery) → return to Tinkee → Egg Collection #4735 (UBRS Rookery again) → Leonid Barthalomew #5522 (Eastern Plaguelands) → Betina Bigglezink #5531 → Dawn’s Gambit #4771. The separate Tinkee Steamboil reminder from Felnok is optional in the historical chain.
- [ ] Horde Shaman-only Darkreaver rows are alternatives, not Warlock quests. Dungeon Set2 Valthalak rows have unconfirmed Forever availability; complete chain shown in the rows, not a standalone Bodley pickup. Your Warlock Imp Delivery #7629 requires60 and is explicitly excluded below.

<a id="shapers-terrace"></a>

## Shaper’s Terrace

### Before you travel

- [ ] Alliance: visit Curator Thorius in Ironforge, Hall of Explorers BEFORE Un’Goro for Panic at the Digsite #95924 and Mapping the Way Forward #95925; take the Cartographer’s Kit. Digestible Research #95790 is a separate chewed-journal record; exact starter location unresolved, collect journal pages inside and check turn-in with Thorius.
- [ ] Horde: visit Master Apothecary Faranell and Chemist Fuely in Undercity’s Apothecarium for The Dark Crystal #95926 and Essence of Seeping Slimes #97049; visit Mayena Earthseeker at Camp Gev’rek, northern Mulgore (about34.9,22.0), for Crystalline Elements #95928. All6 records have minimum55 in Icy Veins zone table. No earlier required chains established, so prerequisite status remains Unknown, not an invented “none”.
- [ ] Bring15 each Red, Yellow, Blue and Green POWER CRYSTALS from Un’Goro for the four console encounters and Nanaya according to Nov13 guide. These are a group resource, NOT the four named crystals for Crystalline Elements. J.D. Collie’s outdoor Crystals of Power consumes7 each if you do it; it is NOT established as mandatory prerequisite to the Terrace quests.
- [ ] Fly to Marshal’s Refuge, Un’Goro Crater, and climb stairs to titan doors above the camp. Older guide’s “no quests” and Mmonster’s single Horde quest are incomplete. WOWF.IO’s generic “inside” labels conflict with named outside givers in direct DB records and Nov13 guide; start with the named NPCs. These new quest offers are sourced, not personally live-verified.

<a id="stratholme-main-gate"></a>

## Stratholme — Main Gate (Live / Scarlet)

### Before you travel

- [ ] Pick up exterior quests in Eastern Plaguelands before entering. Location conflict: current WOWF.IO puts Betina, Leonid, Duke Nicholas and Smokey at Eastwall Tower; Classic-derived guides place them at Light’s Hope Chapel. Check Eastwall Tower first, then the in-game marker. Caretaker Alen’s initial The Restless Souls #5281 is at Light’s Hope; turn it in to Egan at Terrordale, far west, to obtain #5282 AND Egan’s Blaster.
- [ ] One connected instance, two entrances: Main Gate serves Live/Scarlet Bastion; Service Gate serves Undead/Baron. A quest indexed under both is not necessarily completable by either wing alone. Service Gate traditionally needs Key to the City from Magistrate Barthilas; Scarlet Bastion traditionally uses Scarlet Key from Doan’s Strongbox in Scarlet Monastery Library. These key mechanics are Classic-derived unless the party confirms current Forever doors.
- [ ] Cross-wing Aurius chain: loot Medallion of Faith from Malor’s Strongbox in LIVE Scarlet Bastion → return it to Aurius in the chapel → kill UNDEAD Baron with his help → speak to him for Aurius’ Reckoning. Medallion is not a Scholomance or Barov-deeds item.
- [ ] Quest progression needs outside turn-ins: The Flesh Does Not Lie #5212 → turn in to Betina → The Active Agent #5213 (UNDEAD ziggurat); The Archivist #5251 (LIVE) → turn in to Duke BEFORE Balnazzar → Head of Balnazzar starts #5262 → turn in to Duke → Above and Beyond #5263 (UNDEAD Baron). Plan repeat runs or patient-group backtracking.
- [ ] Start with Eva Sarkhoff — Western Plaguelands, Caer Darrow, outside Scholomance: Doctor Theolen Krastinov, the Butcher #5382 → Krastinov’s Bag of Horrors #5515 → Kirtonos the Herald #5384 → equip Spectral Essence → The Human, Ras Frostwhisper #5461 (Magistrate Marduke, Caer Darrow; retrieve Keepsake of Remembrance from ruined buildings in Stromgarde Keep, Arathi Highlands) → The Dying, Ras Frostwhisper #5462 (Marduke sends you to Leonid in Eastern Plaguelands) → Menethil’s Gift #5463 (Leonid) BEFORE entering Stratholme; use ground by UNDEAD Baron, accept #5464 there, return to Leonid for Soulbound Keepsake #5465 and then Marduke for human-Ras Scholo finale.
- [ ] Dungeon Set2/tier0.5: indexed min58 does not establish a usable level59 chain. Availability/phase and root-level gates are unresolved in Forever. Dungeon Set 2 / tier0.5, Classic-derived route (Forever availability and root-level gates unconfirmed): Deliana — Ironforge, High Seat/throne room (Alliance; class-specific An Earnest Proposition) / Mokvar — Orgrimmar, Grommash Hold, Valley of Wisdom (Horde). Complete An Earnest Proposition (bring your class’s Dungeon Set1 bracers) → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan, Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → Just Compensation (Set1 belt and gloves) → In Search of Anthion; use Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer at Stratholme Main Gate to see Anthion Harmon → Dead Man’s Plea #8945 (45-minute Undead Baron rescue) → Proof of Life #8946 (Ysida inside → Anthion outside) → Anthion’s Strange Request → Anthion’s Old Friend → Falrin’s Vendetta (Falrin Treeshaper, Dire Maul Library) → The Instigator’s Enchantment → The Challenge (Theldren in Blackrock Depths arena; turn in to Anthion) → Anthion’s Parting Words (Set1 boots, shoulders, legs) → Bodley’s Unfortunate Fate (capital → Bodley, balcony by Blackrock Spire portal) → Three Kings of Flame (BRD, UBRS, Silithus; Hallowed Brazier from Argent Dawn vendor requires Honored in Classic) → assigned Components of Importance → assigned Left Piece summon → I See Alcaz Island in Your Future... #8970 (Blood Kelp on Alcaz Island) → assigned More Components of Importance → assigned Right Piece summon.
- [ ] Postmaster Malown is a summoned BOSS EVENT, not an omitted pickup quest: kill Stratholme Courier for3 postbox keys, open3 matching postboxes and fight postmen; the third triggers Malown. No external quest chain is documented for this event.
- [ ] For Of Love and Family, start with Tirion Fordring’s cabin by Thondroril River, western Eastern Plaguelands: complete ALL Demon Dogs + Blood Tinged Skies + Carrion Grubbage → Redemption → Of Forgotten Memories (Undercroft) → Of Lost Honor (Northdale) → Of Love and Family #5846 → Artist Renfray at Caer Darrow for #5848. Only then travel for Scarlet Bastion painting.
- [ ] Blacksmith only: arrange the Weaponsmith qualification before Everlook specialization quests (full root in rows). Corruption finishes on Undead side; Sweet Serenity on Scarlet side. These are not generic Warlock quests.

### Quest checklist
