<a id="stratholme-service-gate"></a>

## Stratholme — Service Gate (Undead / Baron)

### Before you travel

- [ ] Pick up exterior quests in Eastern Plaguelands before entering. Location conflict: current WOWF.IO puts Betina, Leonid, Duke Nicholas and Smokey at Eastwall Tower; Classic-derived guides place them at Light’s Hope Chapel. Check Eastwall Tower first, then the in-game marker. Caretaker Alen’s initial The Restless Souls #5281 is at Light’s Hope; turn it in to Egan at Terrordale, far west, to obtain #5282 AND Egan’s Blaster.
- [ ] One connected instance, two entrances: Main Gate serves Live/Scarlet Bastion; Service Gate serves Undead/Baron. A quest indexed under both is not necessarily completable by either wing alone. Service Gate traditionally needs Key to the City from Magistrate Barthilas; Scarlet Bastion traditionally uses Scarlet Key from Doan’s Strongbox in Scarlet Monastery Library. These key mechanics are Classic-derived unless the party confirms current Forever doors.
- [ ] Cross-wing Aurius chain: loot Medallion of Faith from Malor’s Strongbox in LIVE Scarlet Bastion → return it to Aurius in the chapel → kill UNDEAD Baron with his help → speak to him for Aurius’ Reckoning. Medallion is not a Scholomance or Barov-deeds item.
- [ ] Quest progression needs outside turn-ins: The Flesh Does Not Lie #5212 → turn in to Betina → The Active Agent #5213 (UNDEAD ziggurat); The Archivist #5251 (LIVE) → turn in to Duke BEFORE Balnazzar → Head of Balnazzar starts #5262 → turn in to Duke → Above and Beyond #5263 (UNDEAD Baron). Plan repeat runs or patient-group backtracking.
- [ ] Start with Eva Sarkhoff — Western Plaguelands, Caer Darrow, outside Scholomance: Doctor Theolen Krastinov, the Butcher #5382 → Krastinov’s Bag of Horrors #5515 → Kirtonos the Herald #5384 → equip Spectral Essence → The Human, Ras Frostwhisper #5461 (Magistrate Marduke, Caer Darrow; retrieve Keepsake of Remembrance from ruined buildings in Stromgarde Keep, Arathi Highlands) → The Dying, Ras Frostwhisper #5462 (Marduke sends you to Leonid in Eastern Plaguelands) → Menethil’s Gift #5463 (Leonid) BEFORE entering Stratholme; use ground by UNDEAD Baron, accept #5464 there, return to Leonid for Soulbound Keepsake #5465 and then Marduke for human-Ras Scholo finale.
- [ ] Dungeon Set2/tier0.5: indexed min58 does not establish a usable level59 chain. Availability/phase and root-level gates are unresolved in Forever. Dungeon Set 2 / tier0.5, Classic-derived route (Forever availability and root-level gates unconfirmed): Deliana — Ironforge, High Seat/throne room (Alliance; class-specific An Earnest Proposition) / Mokvar — Orgrimmar, Grommash Hold, Valley of Wisdom (Horde). Complete An Earnest Proposition (bring your class’s Dungeon Set1 bracers) → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan, Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → Just Compensation (Set1 belt and gloves) → In Search of Anthion; use Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer at Stratholme Main Gate to see Anthion Harmon → Dead Man’s Plea #8945 (45-minute Undead Baron rescue) → Proof of Life #8946 (Ysida inside → Anthion outside) → Anthion’s Strange Request → Anthion’s Old Friend → Falrin’s Vendetta (Falrin Treeshaper, Dire Maul Library) → The Instigator’s Enchantment → The Challenge (Theldren in Blackrock Depths arena; turn in to Anthion) → Anthion’s Parting Words (Set1 boots, shoulders, legs) → Bodley’s Unfortunate Fate (capital → Bodley, balcony by Blackrock Spire portal) → Three Kings of Flame (BRD, UBRS, Silithus; Hallowed Brazier from Argent Dawn vendor requires Honored in Classic) → assigned Components of Importance → assigned Left Piece summon → I See Alcaz Island in Your Future... #8970 (Blood Kelp on Alcaz Island) → assigned More Components of Importance → assigned Right Piece summon.
- [ ] Postmaster Malown is a summoned BOSS EVENT, not an omitted pickup quest: kill Stratholme Courier for3 postbox keys, open3 matching postboxes and fight postmen; the third triggers Malown. No external quest chain is documented for this event.
- [ ] Horde: visit Nathanos Blightcaller at Marris Stead, Eastern Plaguelands well before the run. Do ALL To Kill With Purpose + Un-Life’s Little Annoyances + The Ranger Lord’s Behest, then BOTH Duskwing, Oh How I Hate Thee... + The Corpulent One to obtain Ramstein. Lady Sylvanas’s Champion of the Banshee Queen from Undercity Royal Quarter is the earliest named lead-in, but its mandatory status is unresolved.
- [ ] Shared Holy Water and flesh-sample quests still work on Undead streets. The Restless Souls #5282 is also listed for this wing by WOWF.IO, though not in Mmonster’s wing index. Do not assume the Main Gate index has exclusive ownership of shared street objectives.

<a id="upper-blackrock-spire"></a>

## Upper Blackrock Spire

### Before you travel

- [ ] Entry/access is separate from quest prerequisites: LBRS itself does not require Seal of Ascension. For UBRS, plan a group keyholder; the two Seal quests start with Vaelan inside LBRS, then require Emberstrife forging in Dustwallow and return to Vaelan. Forever's upper-door enforcement is not independently confirmed. Drakefire Amulet is for Onyxia's Lair, NOT for entering UBRS.
- [ ] Alliance ordinary prework: Count Remington Ridgewell in Stormwind Keep → Mayara Brightwing (#4766) at Morgan's Vigil/Burning Steppes → Doomrigger's Clasp (#4764). For Drakkisath's Demise, first complete #5089 from Wyrmthalak in LBRS and visit Maxwell; do not assume Maxwell offers it on a first Spire visit.
- [ ] Alliance Onyxia full route before final dungeon pickup: Helendis Riverhorn, Morgan's Vigil/Burning Steppes: Dragonkin Menace (#4182) → all six The True Masters steps (Helendis → Magistrate Solomon in Lakeshire/Redridge → Bolvar and Katrana Prestor in Stormwind Keep → Solomon → Marshal Maxwell at Morgan's Vigil; speak to Ragged John at Flame Crest as instructed) → Marshal Windsor (#4241; BRD prison) → Abandoned Hope (#4242; return Maxwell) → A Crumpled Up Note (#4264; BRD drop, take to Windsor) → A Shred of Hope (#4282; BRD Angerforge/Argelmach) → Jail Break! (#4322) → Stormwind Rendezvous (#6402; Squire Rowe at Stormwind gates) → The Great Masquerade (#6403) → The Dragon's Eye (#6501; Bolvar to Haleh at Mazthoril/Winterspring) → Drakefire Amulet (#6502; UBRS Drakkisath). Multiple BRD trips and outdoor turn-ins are required.
- [ ] Both factions, egg chain before travelling: Tinkee Steamboil, Flame Crest/Burning Steppes: Broodling Essence (#4726) → Felnok Steelspring (#4808; Everlook/Winterspring) → Chillwind Horns (#4809; Winterspring) → Return to Tinkee (#4810; Flame Crest) → Tinkee Steamboil (#4907; return to Felnok in Everlook for this referral, then back to Tinkee) → Egg Freezing (#4734; UBRS Rookery). Return to Tinkee after Egg Freezing, accept Egg Collection, and arrange another UBRS Rookery visit. Do not arrive with only Felnok's travel quest.
- [ ] Horde tablet root: Apothecary Zinge in The Apothecarium/Undercity gives Vivian Lagrave and the Darkstone Tablet (#4769) → Shadowmage Vivian Lagrave at Kargath/Badlands gives #4768. Horde Onyxia full route: Warlord Goretooth, Kargath/Badlands: Blood and Thunder (named by Blizzard, ID not established) → Warlord's Command (#4903; item Warlord Goretooth's Command, LBRS Omokk/Voone/Wyrmthalak plus documents) → Eitrigg's Wisdom (#4941; Eitrigg then Thrall, Grommash Hold/Valley of Wisdom, Orgrimmar) → For The Horde! (#4974; Rend). → What the Wind Carries (#6566; Thrall) → The Champion of the Horde (#6567; Rexxar, road through Desolace) → The Testament of Rexxar (#6568; Myranda the Hag, Sorrow Hill/Western Plaguelands) → Oculus Illusions (#6569; Black Dragonspawn Eyes in UBRS). → Emberstrife (#6570; use Amulet of Draconic Subversion, Wyrmbog/Dustwallow Marsh) → all three initial Tests of Skulls (Scryer in Mazthoril/Winterspring, Somnus in Swamp of Sorrows, Chronalis by Caverns of Time/Tanaris) → The Test of Skulls, Axtroz (near Grim Batol/Wetlands) → Ascension... (#6601; Emberstrife to Rexxar) → Blood of the Black Dragon Champion (#6602; return to UBRS for Drakkisath, then Rexxar).
- [ ] Rend wing conflict: Blizzard's Forever article says LBRS for For The Horde!, while indexed dungeon guides and Classic put Rend in UBRS; relocation is not independently established.
- [ ] Blacksmiths: Lorax in Frostwhisper Gorge/Winterspring, Lorax's Tale (#5126) → The Demon Forge (#5127); WOWF reports Blacksmithing270 and precursor class restrictions. Non-smith Warlocks should not assume this is available.
- [ ] Hydraxis prework: Duke Hydraxis on Bay of Storms island/Azshara gives BOTH Poisoned Water (#6804; Eastern Plaguelands) AND Stormers and Rumblers (#6805; Silithus), then Eye of the Emberseer (#6821; min56). Its later Molten Core raid steps are not presently available first-tier content.
- [ ] Get Blackhand's Command item from Scarshield Quartermaster outside the portal before entering; touching Drakkisath's Brand does not require a Blackwing Lair clear. BWL is not announced/currently available as a first-tier Forever raid.
- [ ] Late Set2 prework, only if enabled: Dungeon Set 2 root (matching class/faction, min 58): Deliana, Ironforge/The High Seat throne room (Alliance; Warlock An Earnest Proposition #8911), or Mokvar, Orgrimmar/Grommash Hold in the Valley of Wisdom (Horde; Warlock #8919). An Earnest Proposition → A Supernatural Device → The Ectoplasmic Distiller (Mux Manascrambler, Gadgetzan/Tanaris) → Hunting for Ectoplasm → A Portable Power Source → A Shifty Merchant → Return to Deliana/Mokvar → matching-class Just Compensation → In Search of Anthion → Dead Man's Plea (timed Stratholme rescue) → Proof of Life → Anthion's Strange Request. Use the Extra-Dimensional Ghost Revealer for Anthion outside Stratholme and later Bodley; this is NOT the unrelated Scholomance Spectral Essence. → Anthion's Old Friend (#8948; Anthion outside Stratholme, Eastern Plaguelands) → Falrin's Vendetta (#8949; Falrin, Athenaeum/library). → The Instigator's Enchantment (#8950) → The Challenge (#9015; BRD Ring of the Law) → matching-class Anthion's Parting Words (return to Deliana/Mokvar; Warlock #8958 Alliance/#9021 Horde) → Bodley's Unfortunate Fate (#8960 Alliance/#9032 Horde; Bodley by Blackrock Spire portal) → Three Kings of Flame (#8961: BRD Incendius, UBRS Emberseer, Duke of Cynders in Silithus, Hallowed Brazier) → assigned Components of Importance → assigned Left Piece of Lord Valthalak's Amulet. The Components/Left Piece quests are alternatives, not all required in sequence. → I See Alcaz Island in Your Future... (#8970; Bloodkelp on Alcaz Island, Dustwallow Marsh) → assigned More Components of Importance → assigned Right Piece of Lord Valthalak's Amulet. Left and right assignments may use different dungeons; do not promise both summons on the same visit. → Final Preparations (#8994; Blackrock Bracers and Flask of Supreme Power) → Mea Culpa, Lord Valthalak (#8995; summon in The Beast's room in UBRS). Three Kings of Flame already requires an earlier UBRS Emberseer visit. After Mea Culpa take Return to Bodley (#8996) from the spirit inside before leaving.
- [ ] Inside pickups are not external prequests: Awbee for The Matron Protectorate; Pip/Finkle after skinning The Beast for #5047. These are independent, not a mandatory Awbee → Finkle chain.

## Coverage notes

- City of Dalaran: added Dalaran Patrols (#545), missing from the original dungeon index.
- Dalaran Alliance: treat An Alarming Request as required, consistent with player-observed traces and the user's report; WOWF.IO attunement prose calls it optional, which conflicts.
- Gnomeregan: added repeatable More Sparklematic Action (#4603); equivalent machine variants are noted rather than presented as separate outdoor errands.
- Older dungeon guide tables omit required gates for Highland Hides, Horrors in the Highland, Fallen in the Fen and both new Shaman Elemental Aid quests; corrected from direct quest records and observed traces.
- All 78 eligible indexed dungeon–quest occurrences are retained across the seven assigned sections: RFC10, Hall9, Ruins15, Deadmines9, WC11, SFK8, BFD16. Shared class quests repeat under every relevant dungeon.
- Added Deadmines detonator follow-up92819 from its direct ForeverChanges page. Also retained BFD78916/78917 as explicitly UNKNOWN database-only availability rather than presenting possible Season of Discovery carryovers as guaranteed live quests. Total fragment rows81.
- No assigned indexed quest has minimum acceptance60; excluded_min60 is empty.
- Ruins minimum acceptance15 follows Blizzard Nov2 notes, overriding old 16s. New live Horde prework for92422 and95216 is included despite index/ByteTavern blank prerequisite fields.
- Hall map line96391→96393 is separate from optional three-step Farsen orientation. Important Heirlooms Horde eligibility is disputed (index/Mmonster Both; WOWF.IO Alliance).
- Optional breadcrumbs are distinguished from required chains: Corruption Abroad→Researching, Speak with Shoni→Underground Assault, Trouble in the Deeps→Essence. In Search of Thaelrid→Alliance Villainy remains Required following reviewed current guide/wiki, without a live optional-route trace.
- Rogue minimum1 is preserved as indexed metadata but practical level20 capital mail gate is stated. Horn follow-up78307 pickup is corrected from index inside-dungeon label to Dead Drop reward note; direct objective text is incomplete.
- Paladin acceptance minima1654 and95036 filled as20 from detailed sources. Moon-Kissed Blade giver corrected to Lumina (Trevan gives notes/receives materials). Kor Gem1442 uses direct Forever record Thundris/Auberdine, not conflicting guide Tandaan/Astranaar.
- Toxic Soil Human availability is disputed; current direct chain says Rough Dynamite, older secondary guide says Coarse. No Engineering gate added. No reputation gate documented for assigned quests; no profession gate inferred from quest items or reward patterns.
- Classic-origin chain details are cross-checked against direct quest pages and Forever guides but are not globally live-verified. Questie relation traces are observed inference, not server prerequisite metadata; missing trace entries are not treated as evidence of no prerequisites.
- Wowhead direct quest pages166/1275/1740 returned403; alternative direct ForeverChanges/ForeverEra pages and detailed guides were used. Heart78916/78917 ForeverChanges returned404; retained only as unknown from additional databases.
- Every eligible original-index ID is represented under every assigned indexed wing; wrong wing associations are retained with explicit false-positive notes instead of silently omitted.
- Classic dungeon details are Classic-derived unless specifically sourced to Forever direct records/traces; no claims of live in-game verification. Zul’Farrak guide explicitly admits Classic basis.
- Questie relation traces are observed/inferred relations, not authoritative server rules; full source and conflicts retained. Absence of a trace/chain field is not used as proof of no prerequisite.
- Krol’dok Stronghold: all 12 index quests retained; added Lingering Essence (94997). Added database-only Krol’dok No More! (94969,95056) with unknown availability, explicitly not guaranteed obtainable. WOWF.IO active quest list excludes those two.
- Drowned City: all 8 indexed dungeon quests retained plus sourced post-dungeon followups 95798,95799,96078; complete Alliance Rebel Camp → Southshore root and Horde parallel AND prereqs documented.
- Added optional ZF breadcrumb The Brassbolts Brothers (2769), present in researched dungeon guide but absent index.
- No assigned indexed quest had min acceptance 60; none excluded for min level in these nine sections. Unknown index min levels for Krol’dok were resolved from Forever guides/WOWF.IO (including 96771 min37 versus Icy min38 conflict).
